{"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["A famous open problem in mathematics, the hypothesis stating that, for every finite collection f_1,f_2,…,f_k of non-constant irreducible polynomials over the integers with positive leading coefficients, one of the following conditions holds: (i) there are infinitely many positive integers n such that all of f_1(n),f_2(n),…,f_k(n) are simultaneously prime numbers, or (ii) there is an integer m>1 (called a fixed divisor) which always divides the product f_1(n)f_2(n)⋯f_k(n)."],"topics":["mathematics","number-theory","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":8647813,"upstream_offset":20820657670,"upstream_record_sha256":"95b88ef492eda168c28eaf5758cc37bec9272da4b098f3db8e5ccdb8d75f4b36","word":"Schinzel's hypothesis H"}
